- **fragment-assembly-transformer-architecture**: Use when when you have 1D NMR spectra (1H and/or 13C) of an unknown compound with up to ~19 heavy atoms and need to predict both molecular formula and connectivity without manual structure hypothesis generation. - **fragment-based-count-matrix-generation**: Use when you have a backed AnnData object containing processed fragment data (stored in .obsm['fragment_paired'] or . - **fragment-canonicalization-and-matching**: Use when you have a collection of molecular fragments (e.g., from molecular decomposition, retrosynthesis, or synthetic planning) that must be matched to known fragment libraries or standardized representations before feeding them into a transformer assembly model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-canonicalization-and-matching) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **fragment-counting**: Reference for fragment-level counting in gbcms — R1/R2 quality-weighted consensus, FAD/FAF (fragment alt depth/fraction), QNAME/UMI deduplication, duplex vs singleton UMI families, strand consistency, and amplicon split. Use when touching shared/fragment.rs or any DPF/RDF/ADF logic, or debugging fragment-level VAF. ([msk-access/gbcms](https://github.com/msk-access/gbcms/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/fragment-counting) · ★2)
- **fragment-file-validation**: Use when after invoking pp.make_fragment_file to convert a coordinate-sorted BAM file (e.g., from 10X ATAC or standard alignment) into a compressed fragment file. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/fragment-file-validation) · ★15)
- **fragment-frequency-threshold-optimization**: Use when you have replicate MS/MS spectra with labeled fragment recurrence frequencies and need to select an optimal frequency threshold (beyond the default 0.1) that maximizes spectral quality metrics while minimizing false positive noise. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-frequency-threshold-optimization) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **fragment-intensity-aggregation**: Use when after extracting raw MS/MS spectra from mzML files but before consensus spectrum generation, when you observe high-resolution fragment lists where nearby peaks (within a specified mass tolerance, typically 0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-intensity-aggregation) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **fragment-ion-database-matching**: Use when you have centroid-mode LC-MS AIF chromatograms processed through xcms and RAMClustR, a feature table with target m/z and retention time values, and access to fragment libraries (e.g., LipidPos for lipids). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-ion-database-matching) · ★15 · 3 copies)
- **fragment-ion-difference-counting**: Use when when preparing tandem MS/MS data for spectral alignment and similarity comparison, particularly when you have loaded raw fragmentation spectra and need to extract and quantify mass difference patterns that capture the fragmentation process. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-ion-difference-counting) · ★15 · 3 copies)
- **fragment-ion-library-matching**: Use when you have peak-picked experimental MS/MS data (m/z, retention time, intensity) from UHPLC-HRMS/MS instruments (Orbitrap or Q-TOF from Agilent, Bruker, SCIEX, or similar vendors) and need to annotate detected features with specific lipid identifications using in-silico fragmentation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-ion-library-matching) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **fragment-ion-mass-calibration**: Use when when comparing experimental spectra to reference library spectra and fragment ion m/z values show systematic drift or measurement noise that could distort neutral loss peaks or cosine similarity scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-ion-mass-calibration) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **fragment-ion-mass-matching**: Use when you have a tandem mass spectrum (MSMS) of a known or hypothesized peptide, along with its ProForma 2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-ion-mass-matching) · ★15 · 3 copies)
- **fragment-ion-mass-matching-with-tolerance**: Use when denoising MS/MS spectra and you have: (1) a precursor ion with measured m/z and known molecular formula (from SMILES or direct formula input), (2) an adduct type ([M+H]+, [M+Na]+, etc.), (3) a list of fragment ions with observed m/z values, and (4) a need to distinguish chemically valid. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-ion-mass-matching-with-tolerance) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **fragment-ion-matching**: Use when you have two tandem mass spectra (query and reference) with precursor m/z and fragment ion peaks, and you need to identify which fragment ions correspond between them to assess spectral similarity, detect structural variants, or validate compound identifications. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-ion-matching) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **fragment-ion-peak-annotation**: Use when you have experimental MS/MS spectra matched against a reference library (via cosine similarity or dot-product scoring) and need to map individual fragment peaks in the experimental spectrum to their corresponding m/z and intensity values in the matched library entry. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-ion-peak-annotation) · ★15 · 3 copies)
- **fragment-ion-peak-detection-and-normalization**: Use when immediately after loading raw MS/MS spectra from .mgf, .msp, or .mzML files, before generating the bag-of-fragments corpus or extracting neutral losses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-ion-peak-detection-and-normalization) · ★15 · 3 copies)
- **fragment-ion-scoring-and-ranking**: Use when when you have an experimental MS/MS spectrum (centroid mode) and need to convert it into a scored fragment library entry, or when you must rank candidate fragments by confidence before performing spectrum-to-spectrum matching in metabolite annotation workflows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-ion-scoring-and-ranking) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **fragment-ion-spectral-matching**: Use when after pointwise correlation analysis and exact mass difference refinement have identified candidate ion-species pairs that may share a common analyte origin. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-ion-spectral-matching) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **fragment-ion-theoretical-mass-calculation**: Use when when building a comprehensive lipid fragment ion library covering all chain composition and positional isomer variants (e.g., 168.6 million entries). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-ion-theoretical-mass-calculation) · ★15 · 3 copies)
- **fragment-ion-type-interpretation**: Use when you have an tandem MS spectrum with unidentified peaks and a known or hypothesized peptide sequence (in ProForma 2.0 format, including post-translational modifications). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-ion-type-interpretation) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **fragment-length-estimation-cross-correlation**: Use when when beginning ChIP-Seq analysis with single-end BED/SAM input and no prior knowledge of the library's fragment length. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/fragment-length-estimation-cross-correlation) · ★15)
- **fragment-length-estimation-from-read-pairs**: Use when you have paired-end ChIP-Seq data (BEDPE format) and need to determine the empirical fragment length (insertion length) before peak calling. This is mandatory for paired-end ChIP-Seq workflows to ensure MACS3 correctly extends coverage tracks and calculates local bias; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/fragment-length-estimation-from-read-pairs) · ★15)
- **fragment-length-prediction-and-extension**: Use when after filtering duplicate reads from ChIP-Seq data but before generating pileup coverage tracks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/fragment-length-prediction-and-extension) · ★15)
- **fragment-level-spectrum-prediction**: Use when when you have a molecular structure (SMILES, InChI, or chemical formula) and need to predict its tandem mass spectrum for structural elucidation or mass spectrometry validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-level-spectrum-prediction) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **fragment-mass-tolerance-calibration**: Use when when implementing fragment ion annotation in proteomics workflows and needing to determine whether neutral loss annotation (e.g., H2O: -18.010565, NH3: -17.026549) should be enabled to maximize peak interpretation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-mass-tolerance-calibration) · ★15 · 3 copies)
- **fragment-match-rate-computation**: Use when after denoising MS/MS spectra at multiple frequency thresholds and matching each thresholded spectrum against a -matching reference spectrum. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/fragment-match-rate-computation) · ★15 · 2 copies)
